**Realtime Broadcasting (ADR-047, items 1-3, 2026-08-27):** ✅ `laravel/reverb`/`laravel-echo`/`pusher-js` — `OrderStatusUpdated` (storefront, public `order.{order_number}` channel, `OrderObserver` seam), `PriceSyncRunUpdated` (admin, private `price-sync-run.{id}`, `PriceSyncRunObserver`), `BackupRunUpdated` (admin, private `backups`, `BackupRunObserver`); `docker-compose.prod.yml` `reverb` service + nginx `/app/` proxy; `NEXT_PUBLIC_REVERB_*` build-arg wiring in both Next.js Dockerfiles + CI's `build-and-push`. Not yet real-verified: actual `reverb:start` runtime and the nginx WebSocket handshake — no `.env` access in the build session, build-only (`docker compose build`) verification. **Founder must confirm live before/at first real deploy**, not assumed working from a clean build alone.

**Session summary, 2026-08-27 (new session) — ADR-048 (Queue → Redis, Horizon, Pulse) built end-to-end.** `QUEUE_CONNECTION=redis`, `config/horizon.php`'s three per-queue supervisors (replacing the three `queue-worker-*` containers with one `horizon` service), Redis AOF persistence (`docker-compose.prod.yml`), Pulse adopted alongside. **Grilled and closed live, mid-build, a real gap the ADR's own decision 3 undersold:** this backend has no session-based login anywhere (Sanctum bearer-token-only), so Horizon/Pulse's own `web`-guard dashboards had nothing to authenticate against — a deliberate, scoped exception to the very precedent `ADR-047` decision 3 had just set the same day (avoiding the `web` guard entirely for broadcasting auth). Closed via a signed-URL session bootstrap (`OpsAccessController` — mint a 5-minute signed link from inside the authenticated SPA, visiting it is the *only* place this backend ever calls `Auth::guard('web')->login()`), not a standing login form. Full detail in [ADR-048's build addendum](./adr.md) — not duplicated here. Local dev gained a real new prerequisite (`backend/docker-compose.yml`'s new `redis` service, `docker compose up -d redis` before `composer run dev`) since local dev never previously needed Docker running at all; `AGENTS.md` updated. 901/901 backend suite green (9 new tests, `OpsAccessControllerTest`). `admin/` `tsc --noEmit`/lint clean. **Not done — founder's own next step:** `QUEUE_CONNECTION=redis` still needs setting in the real `backend/.env` (local) and production/staging `.env` files; this assistant's `.env`/`.env.example` file access is permission-blocked. Decision 2's `kill -9`-restart-survival smoke test also still needs running against a real deploy, per the ADR's own consequence-to-track.

**Founder live-testing, same day — one real bug found: `/pulse` 500'd on first real load, first diagnosis wrong, corrected same session.** `Illuminate\Support\Collection` unserialize error. First fix attempt (pointing Pulse's cache driver at `redis`) didn't actually fix it — real cause was `config/cache.php`'s `serializable_classes => false` (a Laravel security default against gadget-chain attacks, a single global setting regardless of store) silently downgrading every cached object to `__PHP_Incomplete_Class`; `laravel/pulse` doesn't account for this newer framework default, a real open upstream gap found via `WebSearch`. Fixed with a narrow allowlist (4 exact types Pulse's source actually caches), not a blanket `true`. Horizon itself tested live and worked correctly first try — real queue job dispatched and observed completing in the dashboard. Full corrected detail in [ADR-048's build addendum](./adr.md).

**Session summary, 2026-08-28 (new session) — MUI-1/MUI-4/MUI-7 grilled and closed as [ADR-052](./adr.md), built same session on `feature/middleware-panel-closure`.** Founder asked for a genuine build-or-drop grilling on these three backlog items rather than building the original wording as-is, since their original scoping rationale was no longer clearly remembered. Investigation before grilling found all three already overlapped shipped screens (Admin Dashboard's System Health, `/middleware/request-logs`, `/middleware/suppliers`, `/admin/games`, the storefront/resend flow's existing validate-player call) — none needed the full original scope. Shipped: MUI-7 as a "Validate Player (by Game)" panel merged onto `/middleware/validators` (`ValidateByGamePanel.tsx`, reuses `validatePlayerForResend()`, zero new backend); MUI-1 as a thin manual-refresh `/middleware` landing page (replacing a years-stale placeholder) reusing `getDashboardHealth()`/`listRequestLogs()`; MUI-4 dropped outright, no screen or export built. `admin/` `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`next build` all clean. Live click-through verification via Chrome automation could not complete this session (mouse clicks stopped registering on the automated tab entirely, an environment/extension issue unrelated to this change; ref-click, coordinate-click, keyboard Tab+Space/Enter, and a full reload were all tried) — **founder manually verified `/middleware` and the new Validators panel live in the browser instead, confirmed working.** PR #16 opened against `staging`.

~~16. **Browser-verify ADR-052's build**...~~ **Done, same day — founder manually verified `/middleware` and the new Validators panel live in the browser, confirmed working.** PR #16 opened against `staging`.
17. **MUI-11/DEV-1-2 (Developer raw API tester)** — the one remaining real gap in the Middleware Panel row, deliberately not bundled into ADR-052's batch (different risk profile: fires raw requests at a live supplier API, needs its own grilling on sandbox-only enforcement / production-mode blocking / its own audit trail).

**Session summary, 2026-08-28 (new session) — Reviews (REV-1..5) grilled and built end-to-end as [ADR-053](./adr.md), on `feature/reviews-management`.** Founder picked this as the next backlog item; grilled from scratch since the PRD's own REV-1..5 wording covers only admin moderation, nothing about how a review actually gets created (guest checkout, ADR-011, no accounts). Real design work: submission gated on `delivery_status=Delivered`, `order_number` as the sole proof of ownership (same trust model as `TrackOrderController`), one review per order (`reviews.order_id` unique index). The load-bearing find: `storefront/src/components/order/OrderStatusTracker.tsx` (ADR-047) already unifies a Reverb push and a poll fallback into one `order` state object — the new review popup (`RateOrderModal.tsx`, deliberately named apart from the pre-existing, unrelated checkout `ReviewModal.tsx`) hooks directly into that existing state via a derived `delivered && !has_review` condition, no new real-time plumbing. `has_review` (new field on `TrackOrderController`'s response and `OrderStatusUpdated`'s broadcast payload) makes "don't show the popup again" a server-truth check, not client-side dismiss-forever state — a customer who closes the popup gets asked again on any later visit, from any device, until they actually submit. Admin side: `/admin/reviews` (stat cards, filterable table, approve/reject, bulk-approve), `average_rating` scoped to approved-only reviews (decision 7) so a rejected review never skews it. No public display anywhere (decision 6) — `TestimonialsSection.tsx`'s static content stays untouched, deliberately deferred to a future ADR if the founder ever wants it. 24 new backend tests (966/966 backend suite green), `admin/`+`storefront/` `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`next build` all clean. **Not yet done this session:** live browser click-through verification (not attempted — see the pointer item below).
